# The values-check of the RESULTS entry for the Q-F0 campaign's launch (PROPOSAL-m v1.11 §8 step 16, m:358). Read-only checks; no agent opened data/m/ckpt/* (shasum and test -e only), the G-F0m-Qwen stage raw or the qualification's report or reads; no contrast (A0) session line read into a return; no stage duration computed or quoted. 2 pass(es), each of three lenses (fidelity, rules and blinding, mechanics), each lens put to a strict refuter: each pass's workflow script, every return verbatim, and Code's dispositions; then the entry's changes by pass. Every return was scanned before assembly for a contrast session line, a PROVENANCE stamp and the sham generation total: none.

====================================================================================================================
PASS 1
====================================================================================================================
---- THE WORKFLOW SCRIPT, verbatim
export const meta = {
  name: 'campaign-launch-entry-vc1',
  description: 'Values-check pass 1 of the Q-F0 campaign launch entry: three lenses, each put to a strict refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Check' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}
const A = args
const CONTEXT = `You are checking a draft RESULTS.md entry for a research repository. Repository: /Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab (git main, HEAD a6c81f2). The draft entry: ${A.entry} (to be inserted at the top of RESULTS.md's "## Log — Phase 2", newest first). It is the launch entry of the Q-F0 campaign (PROPOSAL-m v1.11 §8 step 16), a run that has ended.

Sources (all readable except as the hard rules restrict):
- the campaign raw results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-qf0.txt (untracked, new; its first 13 lines starting '#' are the header Code wrote before the launch);
- the pick-free report data/m/reports/Q-F0_campaign_report.json (untracked, new);
- the launch-checks raw results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-qf0_launchchecks.txt (untracked, new; sections 1-9);
- LOCK-m-F0.md (v1.0, committed at d474406); PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md ("m:N" means line N of this file);
- the handoff README, a scratch copy: ${A.H}/README.md; the dry precheck script ${A.H}/campaign_dry_precheck.py;
- results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-driver_review.txt (the driver's review raw); results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md ("the step-10 read"); results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md ("the step-14 read");
- RESULTS.md (the G-F0m-Qwen entry dated 2026-10-13 is the precedent form for a stage entry; the LOCK v1.0 entry dated 2026-10-16 is the newest);
- scripts/m_sessions.py and valence_core/m/*.py at HEAD; data/m/reports/Q-F0_time_report.json (its "identity" block only);
- Code's scratch scripts under ${A.L} (extract_entry.py, after_exit.sh, gen_header16.py, prelaunch16.sh, cmpcmd.py).

The PI's chat words, verbatim (curly apostrophes and the em dash as typed):
(a) 2026-10-18, the launch: "Audit session is idle; launch the campaign. And yes: at each pause (exit 3), commit the checkpoint and the raw as it stands, by explicit path, with a message naming only the pause’s kind and launch count — no figures, no finish stamp."
(b) 2026-10-19, this entry: "The campaign invocation has ended. Write the launch entry: the self-test of record (825/825) and the dry precheck as run; the launch line as typed, with the header saying whatever was run; the DRIVER sha256 609bb469…2015 (the step-10 read’s Q2); the end state under the lock’s rules — the exit, the launches per arm, the looks and the monitors as the lock lets them print, m:271’s redactions, no finish stamp in the raw or the message. Commit the three outputs by path (the checkpoint, the report, the raw) with the entry and push. If it ended at a pause (exit 3) or a STOP, say which and stop there: the pause comes to the seat. If COMPLETE or CAP, step 17 (VERDICTS) waits on the PI’s word."

HARD RULES (blinding and process; breaking any one fails your check):
1. Never open, read or list anything under data/m/ckpt/. The only permitted operation there is \`shasum -a 256 data/m/ckpt/Q-F0_campaign.json\` and \`test -e\`.
2. Never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt, data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_report.json, data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_reads.json or ADDENDUM-k1.md. Never run git inside jlens-qwen36/.
3. In the campaign raw, never print, quote, count or tally the lines beginning "campaign A0 " (the contrast sessions' printed lines). An A0 figure you need comes from the report, the raw's BAR lines, the launch-checks raw §9, or \`/usr/bin/python3 -I ${A.L}/extract_entry.py results/raw/2026-10-18_m-campaign-qf0.txt\`, which pools a contrast session's valid and excluded@free statuses. Lines beginning "campaign A0s " (sham) and every other line may be read.
4. Never compute or quote a duration or a time span; never quote or use the PROVENANCE line's stamp, the report's sham_generation_ms or the report's provenance values. No ls -l/-t/-s, stat, du, wc -c, find -newer, git ls-tree -l, git cat-file -s or git --stat on the campaign raw, data/m/ckpt or data/m/reports.
5. Read-only: edit no repository file; no git writes (add, commit, checkout, stash, reset, pull, push); no jlens-qwen36 venv Python; no model; no network. Python only as /usr/bin/python3 -I. Scratch files only under ${A.L}/vc1_scratch/.
6. Your return is committed to a record the design seat reads: quote only the entry, the lock, the proposal, the reads, the review raw, and the permitted lines of the raw and report; never an A0 session line, a stamp or a duration.`

const FIND = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string', description: 'what you read and ran, in brief' },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string', description: 'the entry text checked, quoted' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'WRONG', 'UNSUPPORTED', 'FORBIDDEN', 'IMPRECISE', 'MISSING'] },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'none'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'findings'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string' },
  rulings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, upheld: { type: 'boolean' }, rating: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'refuted'] },
    reason: { type: 'string' }, fix_ok: { type: 'string', description: 'is the proposed correction right? give the right one if not' } },
    required: ['id', 'upheld', 'rating', 'reason', 'fix_ok'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'rulings', 'missed'] }

const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', ask: `LENS: FIDELITY. Check every factual claim in the entry against its source: each figure, hash, path, quotation, line number, commit id, section citation and attribution; the PI's words against the verbatim text above, character for character. Cover at least every figure in "The run", "The looks and the monitors", "m:271's redactions", "The self-test … and the dry precheck", "The driver", "The identity" and "The outputs". Return one finding per claim checked, VERIFIED ones included (severity none), ids F1, F2, ….` },
  { key: 'rules', ask: `LENS: RULES AND BLINDING. Check the entry against the rules that bind it: m:263, m:271, m:284, m:318, m:330 and m:358 (steps 16 and 17); the step-10 read's Q2 and Q4; the step-14 read's Q2 and Q3; lock v1.0 §§4, 8, 9, 12 and 13; the handoff README §§2 and 4; and the PI's 2026-10-19 list (b) above, item by item. Flag (FORBIDDEN) anything the entry carries that a rule withholds before VERDICTS (a contrast free-round event, a contrast read after the first, a per-property figure, a duration, a stamp). Flag (WRONG/IMPRECISE) any rule the entry misstates or misattributes, and any precedent or rule it claims that no committed record states. Flag (MISSING) any item the PI listed that the entry omits. Also check that the entry's readings of "verify once", "launch entry", "the window closes here" and "COMPLETE" follow the record. Ids R1, R2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
  { key: 'mechanics', ask: `LENS: MECHANICS. Recompute, with tools: every sha256 the entry names (files on disk; HEAD blobs via git show HEAD:path | shasum -a 256); the 14 PIN line numbers (git show HEAD:scripts/m_sessions.py | grep -n 'declared at the campaign wiring'); every commit id (git cat-file -t); every count, from the report, the raw's BAR lines, the sham session lines and the pooled extraction; the looks' arithmetic (ceil(129/40)); the rotation (A0/A0s alternating for 35 pairs, then A0 alone for 59 — check it with a script that prints only the arm sequence's run lengths, never a status); the line citations (stages.py:1937, m:N lines, lock § numbers, launch-checks raw §§2–4, §6, §7, §9); the links' paths (the values-check raw does not exist yet: note it, do not flag it). Ids M1, M2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
]

const results = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\n${l.ask}\n\nBe exhaustive and exact; a claim you could not check is UNSUPPORTED with the reason.`, { label: `check:${l.key}`, phase: 'Check', schema: FIND }),
  (rep, l) => {
    const open = (rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict !== 'VERIFIED')
    return agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\nYou are a STRICT REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens of this values-check. Its findings that are not VERIFIED follow as JSON. For each, try to refute it from the sources: uphold it only if it is clearly right; rate it fails (the entry states something false or forbidden, or omits a required item), imprecise, note, or refuted; judge its correction (fix_ok). Then look for what the lens missed in its own scope (${l.key}) and return those in missed[] (ids X1, X2, …). The lens also marked ${(rep && rep.findings || []).length - open.length} claims VERIFIED; spot-check at least five of them and put any you overturn in missed[].\n\nThe lens's open findings:\n${JSON.stringify(open, null, 1)}\n\nThe lens's VERIFIED claims (ids and texts):\n${JSON.stringify((rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict === 'VERIFIED').map(f => ({ id: f.id, text: f.text })), null, 1)}`,
      { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF }).then(ref => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: ref }))
  })
return results

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 1, verbatim
Code's dispositions of values-check pass 1. Three lenses with strict refuters; 146 claims; 33 open findings and 19
refuter "missed" items. No figure, hash or count of record was found wrong. Two items were rated fails by a refuter;
both are applied. Every other upheld item is applied as below. The entry's changes are in this raw's last section.

FAILS-CLASS (applied)
- M10 (with F56, R32): the sentence on the keys the report does not carry was false for unit, stage and n.
  Rewritten: the report's identity block (ten keys) is the part compared with G-TIME-F0's. Its top level carries unit
  Q-F0, stage campaign and hour_cap 40, and the identity's per-arm n as schedule_n (stages.py:2348-2350, :2401;
  values A0 2624, A0s 960, from the report). The other resume-identity keys (template_set, stop_cap, key_templates,
  pod_id) are in the checkpoint's identity, which was not opened. "unit Q-F0 (G-TIME-F0's report: Q-F0)" was read by
  Code from that report's top level.
- X1 (mechanics, rules R29, fidelity X1): O2-8's line-form paragraph was missing, though the README's §2 and the
  review raw (:6338) assign it to this entry. Added under "The run", in the precedent's form, with the facts
  launch-checks §9 shows.

IMPRECISE AND NOTES (applied)
- R2 + rules X1: the order as run, and the PI's laptop-return word. That word is now quoted verbatim, from the

const FIND = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string', description: 'what you read and ran, in brief' },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string', description: 'the entry text checked, quoted' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'WRONG', 'UNSUPPORTED', 'FORBIDDEN', 'IMPRECISE', 'MISSING'] },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'none'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'findings'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: {
  checked: { type: 'string' },
  rulings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, upheld: { type: 'boolean' }, rating: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note', 'refuted'] },
    reason: { type: 'string' }, fix_ok: { type: 'string', description: 'is the proposed correction right? give the right one if not' } },
    required: ['id', 'upheld', 'rating', 'reason', 'fix_ok'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['checked', 'rulings', 'missed'] }

const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', ask: `LENS: FIDELITY. Check every factual claim in the entry against its source: each figure, hash, path, quotation, line number, commit id, section citation and attribution; the PI's words against the verbatim text above, character for character. Cover at least every figure in "The run", "The looks and the monitors", "m:271's redactions", "The self-test … and the dry precheck", "The driver", "The identity" and "The outputs". Return one finding per claim checked, VERIFIED ones included (severity none), ids F1, F2, ….` },
  { key: 'rules', ask: `LENS: RULES AND BLINDING. Check the entry against the rules that bind it: m:263, m:271, m:284, m:318, m:330 and m:358 (steps 16 and 17); the step-10 read's Q2 and Q4; the step-14 read's Q2 and Q3; lock v1.0 §§4, 8, 9, 12 and 13; the handoff README §§2 and 4; and the PI's 2026-10-19 list (b) above, item by item. Flag (FORBIDDEN) anything the entry carries that a rule withholds before VERDICTS (a contrast free-round event, a contrast read after the first, a per-property figure, a duration, a stamp). Flag (WRONG/IMPRECISE) any rule the entry misstates or misattributes, and any precedent or rule it claims that no committed record states. Flag (MISSING) any item the PI listed that the entry omits. Also check that the entry's readings of "verify once", "launch entry", "the window closes here" and "COMPLETE" follow the record. Ids R1, R2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
  { key: 'mechanics', ask: `LENS: MECHANICS. Recompute, with tools: every sha256 the entry names (files on disk; HEAD blobs via git show HEAD:path | shasum -a 256); the 14 PIN line numbers (git show HEAD:scripts/m_sessions.py | grep -n 'declared at the campaign wiring'); every commit id (git cat-file -t); every count, from the report, the raw's BAR lines, the sham session lines and the pooled extraction; the looks' arithmetic (ceil(129/40)); the rotation (A0/A0s alternating for 35 pairs, then A0 alone for 59 — check it with a script that prints only the arm sequence's run lengths, never a status); the line citations (stages.py:1937, m:N lines, lock § numbers, launch-checks raw §§2–4, §6, §7, §9); the links' paths (the values-check raw does not exist yet: note it, do not flag it). Ids M1, M2, …; VERIFIED items too (severity none).` },
]

const results = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\n${l.ask}\n\nBe exhaustive and exact; a claim you could not check is UNSUPPORTED with the reason.`, { label: `check:${l.key}`, phase: 'Check', schema: FIND }),
  (rep, l) => {
    const open = (rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict !== 'VERIFIED')
    return agent(`${CONTEXT}\n\nYou are a STRICT REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens of this values-check. Its findings that are not VERIFIED follow as JSON. For each, try to refute it from the sources: uphold it only if it is clearly right; rate it fails (the entry states something false or forbidden, or omits a required item), imprecise, note, or refuted; judge its correction (fix_ok). Then look for what the lens missed in its own scope (${l.key}) and return those in missed[] (ids X1, X2, …). The lens also marked ${(rep && rep.findings || []).length - open.length} claims VERIFIED; spot-check at least five of them and put any you overturn in missed[].\n\nThe lens's open findings:\n${JSON.stringify(open, null, 1)}\n\nThe lens's VERIFIED claims (ids and texts):\n${JSON.stringify((rep && rep.findings || []).filter(f => f.verdict === 'VERIFIED').map(f => ({ id: f.id, text: f.text })), null, 1)}`,
      { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF }).then(ref => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: ref }))
  })
return results

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 2, verbatim
Code's dispositions of values-check pass 2. Three lenses with strict refuters; 174 claims; 28 open findings and 14
refuter "missed" items. No refuter rated anything fails. No figure, hash or count of record was found wrong. Three
findings were refuted (F14, R24, M15) and are not applied. Every upheld item is applied as below. Code alone checked
the edits made after this pass, by the mechanical checks named. The entry's changes are in this raw's last section.

APPLIED
- F23 / R13 / M12: O2-8 is the step-14 review's id (its driver review raw :4552), not the campaign review's. Now
  "(the step-14 review's O2-8; the README's §2)".
- F44 / R11 / M29: Q4's list is given in full: the commit times, the report's PROVENANCE start stamp, and the entry's
  own stamps (here its calendar date alone). This is the refuter's form.

  - §1b's process elapsed times and the venv link's file date;
  - §2's run-time stamps inside the self-test output;
  - §4's pmset readings;
  - §5's and §6's commit times;
  - §7's assertion ages.
